de-dup, keep first occurrence\n    seen = set()\n    out = []\n    for p,u in ns:\n        if (p,u) in seen: continue\n        seen.add((p,u))\n        out.append((p,u))\n    return out\n\n\ndef register_namespaces(ns_list):\n    for prefix, uri in ns_list:\n        ET.register_namespace(prefix, uri)\n\n\ndef paragraph_text(p):\n    # Join all <...:t> under this paragraph, in document order\n    parts = []\n    for node in p.iter():\n        if localname(node.tag) == 't':\n            parts.append(node.text or '')\n    return ''.join(parts)\n\n\ndef remove_descendants_by_localnames(elem, names_set):\n    # remove matching descendants, including direct children\n    stack = [elem]\n    while stack:\n        parent = stack.pop()\n        for child in list(parent):\n            if localname(child.tag) in names_set:\n                parent.remove(child)\n            else:\n                stack.append(child)\n\n\ndef set_paragraph_text_collapse_runs(p, text):\n    # Collapse paragraph to a single <run><t>text</t></run> preserving first run attrs.\n    uri = None\n    if p.tag.startswith('{'):\n        uri = p.tag[1:].split('}',1)[0]\n    run_tag = f'{{{uri}}}run' if uri else 'run'\n    t_tag = f'{{{uri}}}t' if uri else 't'\n\n    children = list(p)\n    runs = [c for c in children if c.tag == run_tag]\n\n    if runs:\n        first_run = runs[0]\n    else:\n        first_run = ET.Element(run_tag)\n        p.insert(0, first_run)\n\n    t = first_run.find(t_tag)\n    if t is None:\n        # remove all children first to avoid mixed content\n        for c in list(first_run):\n            first_run.remove(c)\n        t = ET.SubElement(first_run, t_tag)\n    else:\n        # remove siblings of t inside the run\n        for c in list(first_run):\n            if c is not t:\n                first_run.remove(c)\n\n    t.text = text\n\n    # remove all other run elements under paragraph\n    for r in list(p):\n        if r is first_run:\n            continue\n        if r.tag == run_tag:\n            p.remove(r)\n\n\nwith open(UPDATE_JSON, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    upd = json.load(f)\n\ncustomer = upd['customer']\nowner_name = upd['owner_name']\nwindow_start = upd['window_start']\nwindow_end = upd['window_end']\nband = upd['band']\nescalation_email = upd['escalation_email']\nnote = upd['note']\n\nfollowups = []\nwith open(FOLLOWUPS_CSV, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    for row in reader:\n        if not row: continue\n        followups.append((int(row['sequence']), row['item']))\nfollowups.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\nfollowup_map = {seq: item for seq, item in followups}\n\n# Expect sequences 1..3\nfor expected in (1,2,3):\n    if expected not in followup_map:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"Missing follow-up sequence {expected} in CSV\")\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(SRC_HWPKG, 'r') as zin:\n        zin.extractall(td)\n        infos = zin.infolist()\n\n    # Discover old values by parsing section0.xml and reading relevant paragraphs\n    sec0_path = os.path.join(td, 'Contents', 'section0.xml')\n    ns0 = collect_namespaces(sec0_path)\n    register_namespaces(ns0)\n    t0 = ET.parse(sec0_path)\n    r0 = t0.getroot()\n\n    old_customer = old_owner = old_window_range = old_band = old_escalation = old_note = None\n\n    for p in r0.iter():\n        if localname(p.tag) != 'p':\n            continue\n        txt = paragraph_text(p).strip()\n        if txt.startswith('고객사:'):\n            old_customer = txt.split('고객사:',1)[1].strip()\n        elif txt.startswith('현 담당자:'):\n            old_owner = txt.split('현 담당자:',1)[1].strip()\n        elif txt.startswith('갱신 윈도우:'):\n            old_window_range = txt.split('갱신 윈도우:',1)[1].strip()\n        elif txt.startswith('가격 메모:'):\n            old_note = txt.split('가격 메모:',1)[1].strip()\n\n    # For band/escalation, they are in a table cell paragraph without prefix\n    for p in r0.iter():\n        if localname(p.tag) != 'p':\n            continue\n        txt = paragraph_text(p).strip()\n        if txt and txt == 'Standard-18M':\n            old_band = txt\n        if txt and re.fullmatch(r\"[^@\\s]+@[^@\\s]+\\.[^@\\s]+\", txt):\n            # first email in section0 is escalation\n            if old_escalation is None:\n                old_escalation = txt\n\n    missing = [k for k,v in {\n        'old_customer': old_customer,\n        'old_owner': old_owner,\n        'old_window_range': old_window_range,\n        'old_band': old_band,\n        'old_escalation': old_escalation,\n        'old_note': old_note,\n    }.items() if v is None]\n    if missing:\n        raise SystemExit('Could not detect old values: ' + ', '.join(missing))\n\n    appendix_sentence = '이 부록 문단은 그대로 유지해야 합니다.'\n\n    modified_paras = []  # (xml_path, p_id)\n\n    def process_xml(xml_path: str):\n        ns = collect_namespaces(xml_path)\n        register_namespaces(ns)\n        tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n        root = tree.getroot()\n\n        # track appendix existence in section1\n        appendix_found = False\n\n        for p in root.iter():\n            if localname(p.tag) != 'p':\n                continue\n            before = paragraph_text(p)\n            after = before\n\n            # follow-ups replacement\n            m = re.match(r\"^\\s*([1-3])\\.\\s*(.*)$\", before)\n            if m and 'old follow-up' in before:\n                n = int(m.group(1))\n                after = f\"{n}. {followup_map[n]}\"\n\n            # field replacements (do range first so we don't accidentally replace date fragments)\n            if old_window_range in after:\n                after = after.replace(old_window_range, f\"{window_start} ~ {window_end}\")\n            if old_customer in after:\n                after = after.replace(old_customer, customer)\n            if old_owner in after:\n                after = after.replace(old_owner, owner_name)\n            if old_band in after:\n                after = after.replace(old_band, band)\n            if old_escalation in after:\n                after = after.replace(old_escalation, escalation_email)\n            if old_note in after:\n                after = after.replace(old_note, note)\n\n            if appendix_sentence in before:\n                appendix_found = True\n                #
